# Phase 9.6 Copy-First Goal Tracker

**Created:** 2026-05-13
**Status:** active
**Purpose:** durable goal override and implementation tracker for the copy-first replacement. The Codex goal tool objective cannot be edited in place, so this file is the tracked goal state agents must follow.

## Corrected Goal

Fulcrum must become a full agent-native project/workflow system by preserving and adapting the workflows, UX behavior, data models, tests, and feature value from:

- Plannotator: planning, annotation, review, UAT/code-review feedback loops.
- Plane: full project-management model and UX for workspaces, projects, issues/tasks, cycles, modules, views, intake, labels, notifications, boards, lists, and command navigation.
- Fusion: live agent execution, dependency-aware task runs, approvals, lineage, activity feeds, runtime status, recovery, and animated/live updates.
- Docmost: docs/workspace UX, editor, page tree, backlinks, comments, history, attachments, collaboration, and search.
- ACP UI: ACP sessions, permissions, traffic, model/mode/agent selectors, transports, host/storage, and guided planning UI.

The implementation must copy/adapt upstream code into Fulcrum's real structure only after the workflow/value/data/test mapping is written. Upstream repos and baseline archives remain preserved in ignored scratch storage until cleanup is explicitly approved.

## Goal Rules

- `AGPL/relicense/legal/about` work is not the current priority and must not lead the restart.
- No tracked `vendor-src/**` or equivalent upstream archive folder is allowed.
- No deletion of `.scratch/upstream-product-replacement/**` until the complete mapping and workflow verification are accepted.
- Copy/adapt files only into real Fulcrum paths: `apps/**`, `services/**`, `src/**`, `tests/**`, migrations, package manifests, and supporting build config.
- Every copied/adapted behavior must be traceable through the planning/copy matrix docs: upstream repo, commit SHA, original path, local target path, license, and adaptation notes. Runtime code must not carry provenance headers or source-product names unless the file is a real third-party integration boundary.
- Business logic and persistence must live in bounded services and application services. Web, CLI, and TUI remain invocation/visualization layers.
- Server target: NestJS defaults, `@nestjs/typeorm`, `typeorm`, Express adapter, OpenAPI, class-validator.
- Database target: PostgreSQL production, PGlite local/test.
- Database switching rule, effective now: Nest/TypeORM startup defaults to managed local PGlite under `FULCRUM_HOME/pglite.data` when no database URL is configured, switches to normal PostgreSQL through `FULCRUM_DATABASE_URL` or `DATABASE_URL` without code changes, and may use `FULCRUM_TYPEORM_PGLITE_SOCKET_URL` only for an explicit prestarted PGlite socket.
- Single persistence/server stack rule, effective now: no `.sql` migration files, no dual ORM runtime, and no parallel HTTP/API server framework beside NestJS. TypeScript TypeORM migration classes are the final migration target. Hono, server-side tRPC, MikroORM, and Kysely are removal debt unless a temporary bridge is explicitly documented with owner, blocker, and deletion criteria.
- NestJS standard rule, effective now: server features must be Nest modules with controllers, providers/application services, DTOs, class-validator validation, guards/interceptors where appropriate, Swagger/OpenAPI through `@nestjs/swagger`, and TypeORM integration through service infrastructure or injected providers. Standalone route trees/adapters and app-layer database logic are not accepted final structure.
- Dependency consolidation rule, effective now: use one dependency per need. Duplicate routers, ORMs, migration systems, schema tools, queue engines, state libraries, or API frameworks are architecture debt until removed or explicitly justified.
- Runtime target: keep Bun where practical; switch to pnpm only if the mapped Nest/React integration requires it.
- Naming rule, effective now: do not introduce goal, phase, plan-progress, or third-party app/tool names in code symbols, code comments, user-facing code labels, or code file names. Code names must describe the module responsibility, feature value, or behavior. Third-party names belong in docs/README/provenance manifests unless the file is truly an integration for that third-party tool.
- Existing code and file names must pass `tests/infrastructure/responsibility-first-naming.test.ts` before new workflow progress is accepted. Do not defer new source-product, phase, goal, or plan-progress labels into later cleanup.
- Structural rule, effective now: Fulcrum product code must move toward service-oriented DDD boundaries. Runtime code belongs under bounded services/domains with `domain`, `application`, `infrastructure`, and `interface` responsibilities clear enough that a service can later split into its own package, process, or repository. Do not create catch-all copied-source, upstream-derived, phase-named, or goal-named code/test folders.
- Cleanup gate: before this goal can be complete, run a naming/structure cleanup pass over old and new code. Remove or rename code/test paths and symbols that reference phases, goals, copied-source progress, or third-party inspiration unless the file is a true third-party integration boundary.
- Final clean-standard gate: before this goal can be complete, the tracked repository must have no Hono runtime, no server-side tRPC runtime, no MikroORM/Kysely runtime or tests, no `.sql` migration files, no empty runtime folders, no scattered root code buckets, no duplicate compatibility folders without a deletion owner, and no test/code paths named for phases/goals/source-product provenance.

## Current Authority For Agents

The current runtime structure is service-oriented DDD. Historical evidence sections below may mention source projects, copied-source labels, or old paths for provenance and traceability only. They are not instructions for new code. If a historical evidence path conflicts with the current service map in this section, the current service map wins.

New or moved product code must use these boundaries:

- `services/agent-client-protocol/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/identity-access/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/integration-hub/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/knowledge-workspace/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/notification-center/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/platform-core/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/work-management/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/planning-review/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/execution-orchestration/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.
- `services/workflow-coordination/src/{domain,application,infrastructure,interface}/**` as needed.

Current concrete service roots already in use:

- `services/agent-client-protocol/src/domain/**`, `services/agent-client-protocol/src/application/**`
- `services/identity-access/src/domain/**`, `services/identity-access/src/application/**`
- `services/integration-hub/src/domain/**`, `services/integration-hub/src/application/**`
- `services/knowledge-workspace/src/domain/**`, `services/knowledge-workspace/src/application/**`, `services/knowledge-workspace/src/infrastructure/database/**`
- `services/notification-center/src/domain/**`, `services/notification-center/src/application/**`
- `services/platform-core/src/domain/**`, `services/platform-core/src/application/**`, `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/database/**`, `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/doctor/**`, `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/product-store/**`, `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/skill-supply/**`
- `services/work-management/src/domain/**`, `services/work-management/src/application/**`, `services/work-management/src/infrastructure/database/**`
- `services/planning-review/src/domain/**`, `services/planning-review/src/application/**`, `services/planning-review/src/infrastructure/database/**`
- `services/execution-orchestration/src/domain/**`, `services/execution-orchestration/src/application/**`, `services/execution-orchestration/src/infrastructure/database/**`
- `services/workflow-coordination/src/domain/**`, `services/workflow-coordination/src/application/**`, `services/workflow-coordination/src/interface/http/**`, `services/workflow-coordination/src/infrastructure/database/**`

`src/application/**` is no longer an active product-code root. `apps/server` is a Nest/Express composition root and must not own database implementation folders; TypeORM boot/data-source helpers live in service infrastructure. Current copied-behavior tests live under `tests/services/**` or beside the owning service/application slice. Do not recreate `tests/upstream-derived/**`, `vendor-src/**`, source-product runtime folders, `copy-first-*` runtime modules, or phase/goal-named code paths. Runtime OpenAPI descriptions, code comments, tests, and UI labels must also avoid source-product/progress phrasing unless the file is a real external integration boundary.

## Required End-To-End Workflow

Status semantics: `Mapped, not implemented` means upstream value and Fulcrum integration anchors are documented in `09.6-WORKFLOW-VALUE-MAP.md`. It does not mean the workflow works yet.

| ID | Workflow value | Required outcome | Upstream value source | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| W1 | Start new work from freeform docs | User can create/collect freeform docs as initial context for work | Docmost docs, Fulcrum current docs/context | Partial persisted trace-linked freeform intake action creates a Docmost-style scratch doc, records `freeform_work_started`, builds ACP/Plannotator planning context from that doc, and exposes web/tRPC/CLI/TUI entry points; Docmost-derived TypeORM mirrors now persist page tree/history/comments/attachments/backlinks/collaboration/search on PGlite and real PostgreSQL; first Nest Docmost page-tree/page-operations preview routes/services are wired, the Nest planning freeform start route persists a freeform source doc into TypeORM `fulcrum_documents`, and the public docs API now covers create/edit/detail/global-tree, comments, backlinks, attachment metadata, deterministic search-entry sync, and collaboration-state list/update/delete before returning ACP/Plannotator context; final React docs editor/workbench and service cutover remain pending |
| W2 | Start new work from guided ACP UI | User can use ACP sessions, prompts, model/mode/agent selectors, permissions, and traffic visibility | ACP UI | Partial persisted guided ACP planning action starts trace-linked session state from agent/cwd/template/mode/model/permission/doc inputs, records ACP traffic, emits `acp_guided_planning_started`, and exposes web/tRPC/CLI/TUI entry points; the Nest guided ACP start route persists `fulcrum_acp_sessions` with mode/model/permission metadata and traffic log; guided ACP session actions now record resume, cancel, permission resolution/cancellation, mode changes, and model changes through the workflow API with web/CLI/TUI controls; final React ACP session UI, live transport process bridge, runtime permission dialog loop, and broader Nest/service cutover remain pending |
| W3 | Freeform context feeds later ACP planning | Freeform docs are available as context when creating plans/tasks through ACP UI | ACP UI + Docmost + Fulcrum context | Partial persisted application/API path with web/CLI/TUI prompt/freeform-start/guided-ACP entry points; Docmost-derived page/backlink/search/collaboration mirrors plus deterministic context-bundle/memory TypeORM mirrors now provide persisted context substrate, and Nest context trace, Docmost page-tree/page-operations previews, and planning freeform/guided/continuous-update APIs persist/load that substrate; final React ACP session UI, automatic caller cutover, and deeper task reconciliation UX remain pending |
| W4 | Technical planning with prototype/boilerplate | Planning produces deep technical plans plus prototype/boilerplate artifacts, not docs-only output | Plannotator + Fulcrum artifacts | Partial generated technical-planning service builds reviewable plan markdown from freeform/guided/update context, attaches prototype and boilerplate paths plus success criteria, returns an approved-plan-compatible task breakdown, emits `technical_planning_generated`, exposes tRPC, CLI, web, and TUI entry points, persists artifact preview/run metadata on TypeORM prototype rows, records reviewable artifact execution attempts/status through a Nest/service route, can run artifact commands through the service-owned sandbox runner with persisted success/failure/blocked/ready history, and exposes run controls/history through current web, generated CLI, and TUI planning surfaces; final React review UX remains pending |
| W5 | Review planning/prototypes in feedback loops | User can annotate, suggest, approve, or reject plan/prototype/code/UAT material | Plannotator review editor | Partial Plannotator-derived review workbench model plus persisted versioned review-session state for diff file tree, annotation grouping, suggestions, search, feedback export, submission targets/orphans, live logs, trace/review IDs, persisted inline annotation append revisions, web/tRPC/CLI/TUI entry points, Nest review-workbench preview plus review-session save/load/annotate route/services over TypeORM rows, and current-stack Svelte Plannotator-style review editor results for both direct review workbench builds and loaded/saved/annotated review sessions with file tree, selected diff pane, annotation sidebar, AI/Agents tab shells, search dock, submission/feedback export dock, and live-log dock; final React review editor, interactive inline annotation selection controls, real Dockview panels, and live AI/agent review sessions remain pending |
| W6 | Approved plan becomes docs and PM breakdown | Approved plan writes docs and decomposes into goals, phases/cycles, tasks, dependencies, success criteria | Plane + Docmost + Plannotator + Fusion | Partial persisted application path with shared tRPC adapter plus web/CLI/TUI preview/materialize entry points; Nest approved-plan preview and materialize routes/services now delegate to the copied Plannotator/Plane/Docmost/Fusion breakdown model and persist generated docs, tasks, dependencies, artifacts, plans, and prototypes through copy-first TypeORM tables; broader Nest/service cutover remains pending |
| W7 | Full PM system works without AI | Workspace/project/task/cycle/module/view/intake/board/list/table workflows are usable manually | Plane | Partial Plane-derived manual workbench action builds board/list/table snapshots over real tasks with Plane state groups, filter counts, access labels, table columns, trace IDs, and web/tRPC/CLI/TUI entry points; core work-item commands, queries, schemas, CSV import/export, task-detail relationship behavior, work-item task action behavior, work-item relationship behavior, work-item comment/watch/reaction behavior, workflow-rule behavior, field dependency behavior, template behavior, automation behavior, recurrence behavior, work-metrics/report behavior, and work-cycle command/query/service behavior now live under the work-management service with legacy task/sprint/workflow/comment/relationship/field-dependency/template/automation/recurrence/report paths reduced to compatibility re-exports; TypeORM mirrors now persist Plane state/label/cycle/module/saved-view/intake/notification records on PGlite and real PostgreSQL; full workspace/project/module/view/intake CRUD, final React workbench, and Nest API/UI parity remain pending |

| W11 | Final QA against docs/success criteria | Final full QA/review checks explicit docs and criteria before user handoff | Plannotator + Fulcrum reports | Partial persisted final-QA report action with docs/tasks/runs/artifacts/success-criteria traversal, dependency/open-feedback/run-status checks, trace-linked `final_qa_completed` event, web/tRPC/CLI/TUI entry points, Nest final-QA route/service parity over TypeORM docs/tasks/runs/artifacts/run-events with PGlite integration coverage, and current-stack final-QA feedback gate that runs the automated feedback loop when open feedback blocks handoff, triggers the default live reviewer-agent path when needed, rebuilds final QA after the loop, blocks truthful handoff when the reviewer is unavailable/feedback fails/max iteration is reached, emits `final_qa_feedback_gate_completed`, and exposes thin tRPC, web, CLI, and TUI actions; final React reports/workflow surface remains pending |

| W14 | Continuous update loop | ACP edits or manual/freeform doc edits can restart the cycle with updated context/tasks | ACP UI + Docmost + Plane | Partial persisted continuous-update action updates or creates Docmost-style docs from manual edit/ACP-session triggers, rebuilds ACP/Plannotator planning context with selected docs and Plane task reconciliation context, emits `planning_cycle_restarted`, and exposes web/tRPC/CLI/TUI entry points; the Nest continuous-update route/service now persists changed TypeORM docs, updates ACP session traffic, and returns task dependency context; final React docs/ACP edit UI, automatic task reconciliation, and broader service cutover remain pending |

## 2026-05-15 Copy/Adapt Completeness Audit

This audit is the current working checkpoint while the separate TypeORM/Nest migration lane runs in Claude. Do not edit or resolve that lane from this goal slice. Recheck it later against `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-05-15-mikroorm-to-typeorm-migration.md` and `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-15-mikroorm-to-typeorm-migration-design.md`.

| Scope | Preserved value now | Missing value before this goal can close | Next implementation slice |
|---|---|---|---|
| W1 docs intake | Trace-linked freeform intake, document context assembly, page-tree/page-operation previews, current document create/edit/detail/global-tree mutations and views through the public document API, current generated CLI document commands through the public API, current TUI document metadata reader through the public API overlay, current document-detail backlinks/comments with create/resolve comment actions, document attachment metadata create/list/delete through the public API with PGlite/PostgreSQL persistence, deterministic search-entry sync for public document create/update/delete, collaboration-state list/update/delete through the public API, and TypeORM mirrors for page tree/history/comments/attachments/backlinks/collaboration/search | Full docs workbench/editor UX, binary attachment upload/download integration, final route/service cutover | Complete knowledge-workspace docs editor/API surface and route all remaining doc workbench mutations through it |
| W2 guided agent UI | Session metadata, model/mode/agent/cwd/permission selections, traffic log, guided planning start across web/CLI/TUI, runtime permission requests flowing from bridge to session state, service-level session resume via ACP load-session, stdio/websocket bridge factory for live local/remote agents, a service-owned session workbench model for connection state/selectors/permissions/messages/tool calls/traffic counts/resumable sessions/traffic monitor state, current-stack web panel rendering that model on the agents page, and planning-surface guided session actions for resume/cancel/permission/mode/model traffic across web/CLI/TUI | Traffic streaming into final React session UI, live bridge execution connected to persisted planning sessions, and final React permission/action dialogs | Connect traffic streaming through service interfaces, connect live bridge sessions to persisted planning sessions, then replace the current-stack panel with the final React session workbench |
| W3 context to planning | Selected documents and trace-linked context flow into planning prompts and continuous update APIs, with current-stack planning results rendering selected context document titles/breadcrumbs/version/truncation metadata plus continuous-update task reconciliation state | Automatic caller cutover and final reconciliation actions in the React planning workbench | Add final reconciliation actions in the planning workbench after remaining caller cutover |
| W4 technical planning | Technical plan generation with prototype/boilerplate paths, success criteria, review IDs, task-breakdown payload, reviewable artifact preview/run metadata, persisted preview metadata, append-only artifact execution records on TypeORM prototype rows, service-owned sandbox artifact command runs exposed through the workflow API caller, and current web/CLI/TUI planning run controls/history | Final React review workbench cutover for prototype/code review | Move the current execution-history/run-control value into the final React review workbench during the UI replacement |
| W5 review feedback | Review model, diff tree, annotation grouping, suggestions, search, feedback export, persisted review sessions, Svelte current-stack review surface | React review editor, interactive inline selection, real dock panels, live AI/reviewer sessions | Replace current review surface with React workbench while keeping review business logic in planning-review services |
| W6 approved plan materialization | Approved plan can create docs, artifacts, tasks, dependencies, success criteria, trace IDs through shared services | Full cutover from compatibility/current-stack route adapters to standard service API surfaces | Move remaining materialization callers behind Nest/service interfaces after the migration lane stabilizes |
| W7 manual project management | Board/list/table snapshots, state groups, filters, task actions, relationships, comments, workflow rules, work-cycle behavior, TypeORM mirror records | Full workspace/project/module/view/intake CRUD, command navigation, issue detail parity, React workbench, Nest API/UI parity | Copy/adapt remaining PM model screens and command/navigation behavior into work-management service plus React workbench |
| W8 run controls | Board/task dependency-run preview and dispatch with trace-linked dependency disclosure across web/CLI/TUI | Final React PM run controls and graph disclosure in the PM surface | Add dependency-run controls to the final PM workbench components |
| W9 dependency execution | Dependency ordering, queued runs/jobs, worker tick, lifecycle events, live feedback streams, CLI watch, TUI feedback, web EventSource panel | Final React PM surface integration and broader service cutover | Attach run lifecycle feedback to the final PM workbench and remove current-stack compatibility callers |
| W10 QA loops | Success-criteria extraction, dependency-gated approval, corrective feedback runs, automated feedback-loop exhaustion service | Dedicated React live-review controls and operational run controls for loop status | Build live review controls backed by execution-orchestration interfaces |
| W11 final QA | Final QA report, feedback gate, trace-linked pass/fail status, web/CLI/TUI entry points | Final React reports/workflow surface | Replace reports surface with final React workbench while preserving service-owned report logic |
| W12 UAT/code review | Handoff, manual decisions, auto-decision, generated E2E actions, persisted review sessions, current Svelte review value | React UAT/review editor, inline annotation interactions, dock panels, live AI/reviewer sessions | Finish React review/UAT workbench and connect live sessions through service APIs |
| W13 real-data E2E | Accepted generated regression artifacts, per-task coverage cases, artifact materialization, Bun/Playwright runner planning, local CI step | Final React UAT surface cutover and full end-to-end proof with no mocked UAT data | Run a real-data acceptance-cycle fixture through web/CLI/TUI after UI cutover |
| W14 continuous updates | Manual doc/ACP updates rebuild planning context and target-task dependency context with trace events | Final docs/ACP edit UI, automatic task reconciliation, broader service cutover | Add edit/resume controls and reconciliation actions to the docs/planning workbenches |
| Server/persistence lane | PGlite/PostgreSQL switching and several TypeORM service mirrors already have focused evidence | Full removal of MikroORM/Kysely/Hono/server-side tRPC/runtime dual migration systems and all `.sql` migrations | Defer to the Claude-owned migration plan; rerun final architecture scans after Claude finishes |
| Repository structure | Service interface boundary work has removed route-level direct application imports from current web/CLI/TUI scans | Empty/runtime-folder cleanup, compatibility export deletion owners, final service split-readiness audit | Run cleanup pass after the migration lane and UI cutovers settle |

Acceptance impact: keep the final criteria for "each upstream workflow value preserved", "copy/adapt completeness audited", "NestJS + TypeORM replacement", "repository structure clean", and "full end-to-end loop passes" unchecked. This audit proves what remains; it does not prove completion.

## Phase 4 Partial Implementation Evidence

- ACP UI `src/lib/types.ts`, `src/stores/traffic.ts`, `src/lib/transport/types.ts`, `src/lib/transport/websocket.ts`, and `src/lib/acp-bridge.ts` copied/adapted into `src/application/acp/**`.
- ACP UI `src/stores/config.ts` and `src/stores/session.ts` state behavior ported into `src/application/acp/{config-store,session-store}.ts`.
- ACP UI `src/stores/session.ts` create-session, prompt, mode/model, permission, disconnect, and unexpected-close behavior ported into `src/application/acp/session-manager.ts`.
- Copied/adapted ACP production files include provenance headers with upstream repo, commit SHA, original path, license, local target path, and adaptation notes.
- `tests/upstream-derived/acp/acp-bridge.test.ts` preserves upstream-derived behavior for transport config guards, listener mutation safety, traffic recording, JSON-RPC requests/responses, host filesystem method-not-found behavior, permission request resolution, unexpected transport close rejection, and WebSocket bearer subprotocol handling.
- `tests/upstream-derived/acp/session-config-store.test.ts` preserves upstream-derived behavior for config hot reload/filtering, startup phase detection, session/update message merging, tool call updates, mode updates, slash commands, resumable sessions, and disconnect state clearing.
- `tests/upstream-derived/acp/session-manager.test.ts` preserves upstream-derived behavior for session initialize/new, load-session capability, modes/models, bridge update wiring, prompt forwarding, mode/model/cancel/permission proxies, unexpected close, and disconnect cleanup.
- This is only an ACP protocol foundation plus the first persisted guided-planning start, Nest guided-start API, continuous-update action, and Nest continuous-update route. W2 is partial, not complete, until the final React ACP session UI, live transport process bridge, runtime permission dialog loop, traffic monitor UI, and broader service cutover land. W14 is partial, not complete, until final React docs/ACP edit UI, automatic task reconciliation, and broader service cutover land. W3 has persisted planning-context, guided-ACP, update/replan entry paths, and Nest freeform/guided/continuous APIs but still needs final React ACP session UI.

### Plannotator Planning Primitives

- Plannotator `apps/opencode-plugin/plan-mode.ts` copied/adapted into `src/application/planning/plan-mode.ts`.
- Plannotator `apps/opencode-plugin/workflow.ts` copied/adapted into `src/application/planning/plannotator-workflow.ts`.
- Plannotator `apps/opencode-plugin/index.ts` submit-plan text/path detection, planning prompt, planning-agent guard, and approval/denial prompt behavior ported into `src/application/planning/plan-session.ts`.
- Docmost breadcrumb/tree semantics, ACP prompt flow, and Plannotator planning prompt copied/adapted into `src/application/planning/freeform-doc-context.ts`.
- Shared persisted-doc bridge added through `src/application/planning/freeform-doc-actions.ts`; it loads real Fulcrum docs through application queries, maps them to Docmost tree context, and builds an ACP/Plannotator prompt with selected doc refs and trace metadata.
- Continuous update bridge added through `src/application/planning/continuous-update-actions.ts`; it updates or creates Docmost-style docs from manual/freeform edits or ACP session updates, rebuilds ACP/Plannotator planning context, loads Plane target task status/dependency/success context, records missing target IDs, and emits a trace-linked `planning_cycle_restarted` event.
- Service-owned technical planning generation now lives in `services/planning-review/src/application/technical-planning-cycle.ts`; it builds reviewable technical plan markdown from selected docs, preserves trace/source metadata, attaches prototype and boilerplate artifact paths, generates task success criteria, returns an approved-plan-compatible breakdown, and records `technical_planning_generated`.
- The technical planning generation path is exposed through `planning.generateTechnicalPlanningCycle`, `fulcrum product planning generate`, the `/planning` web form/action/result, and the TUI planning screen `g` action without moving generation logic into web, CLI, or TUI.
- Plannotator approved plan/session behavior, Fusion planning subtask breakdown/materialization sequencing, Plane issue/cycle/module task fields, Docmost doc/source-link semantics, and Fulcrum artifact lifecycle metadata copied/adapted into `src/application/planning/approved-plan-breakdown.ts`.
- Existing Fulcrum docs/artifacts/tasks application commands wired through `materializeApprovedPlanBreakdownWithApplicationCommands(...)` so approved breakdowns persist real Docmost-style docs, prototype/boilerplate artifact rows, Plane-style tasks, and Fusion-style dependencies through shared services.
- Shared current-stack server adapter added through `src/application/planning/approved-plan-actions.ts` and `apps/server/src/runtime/trpc/routers/planning.ts`, exposing `planning.buildFreeformDocsPlanningPrompt`, `planning.restartPlanningCycleFromUpdates`, `planning.previewApprovedPlanBreakdown`, and `planning.materializeApprovedPlanBreakdown` to web/CLI/TUI callers without duplicating planning/doc-loading/persistence logic in clients.
- Nest `workflows/copy-first/planning/approved-plan/materialize` route and service were added through `apps/server/src/workflows/{copy-first-planning.controller.ts,copy-first-planning.service.ts}`; they materialize the copied approved-plan breakdown into copy-first TypeORM docs, tasks, dependencies, artifacts, plan, and prototype rows through PGlite socket, without relying on web/CLI/TUI business logic.
- Nest `workflows/copy-first/planning/freeform/start` and `workflows/copy-first/planning/guided-acp/start` routes/services were added through `apps/server/src/workflows/{copy-first-planning.controller.ts,copy-first-planning.service.ts}`; they persist copy-first TypeORM freeform docs and ACP sessions, rebuild ACP/Plannotator context from TypeORM docs, preserve trace/mode/model/permission metadata, and record ACP traffic logs through PGlite socket.
- Nest `workflows/copy-first/planning/continuous-update/restart` route/service was added through `apps/server/src/workflows/{copy-first-planning.controller.ts,copy-first-planning.service.ts}`; it persists changed TypeORM docs, rebuilds ACP/Plannotator context from selected docs, loads TypeORM task dependency context, appends ACP session traffic, and returns missing target IDs through PGlite socket.
- Web `/planning` route added through `apps/web/src/routes/planning/+page.server.ts` and `apps/web/src/routes/planning/+page.svelte`; it submits approved markdown, plan/source/trace/project fields, and source doc refs to the shared planning tRPC procedures and renders returned docs/tasks/success criteria/warnings without local planning business logic.
- Web `/planning` also submits freeform prompt, selected doc IDs, trace, project, and max-doc-char inputs to `planning.buildFreeformDocsPlanningPrompt` and renders the returned ACP planning prompt/source refs from server results only.
- Web `/planning` also submits continuous-update trigger, changed doc, selected doc, target task, trace, ACP session, mode/model, project, and max-doc-char inputs to `planning.restartPlanningCycleFromUpdates` and renders the returned replan prompt/source refs from server results only.
- CLI `fulcrum product planning preview/materialize/freeform-prompt/continuous-update` added through `apps/cli/src/product.ts`; it reads the approved plan markdown file when needed, preserves project/trace/review/cycle/module/source-doc/freeform/update fields, and delegates to the shared planning caller.
- TUI `PlanningBreakdownScreen` added through `apps/tui/src/screens/planning-breakdown.ts` and wired into root navigation through `apps/tui/src/index.ts`; it calls `caller.planning.previewApprovedPlanBreakdown(...)`, `caller.planning.materializeApprovedPlanBreakdown(...)`, `caller.planning.buildFreeformDocsPlanningPrompt(...)`, and `caller.planning.restartPlanningCycleFromUpdates(...)` and only renders returned docs/tasks/blockers/warnings/materialized IDs/freeform/continuous-update prompt content.
- Plannotator `packages/shared/prompts.ts` plan prompt builders ported into `src/application/planning/plannotator-prompts.ts`.
- Plannotator `packages/shared/feedback-templates.ts` plan-denial feedback template copied into `src/application/reviews/shared/feedback-templates.ts`.

- `apps/web/src/routes/planning/page.svelte.test.ts` verifies the web route renders preview/materialize/freeform/guided-ACP/continuous-update controls and trace-linked docs/tasks/warnings/freeform/replan prompt output from server action results.
- `services/planning-review/src/application/artifact-execution.ts`, `services/workflow-coordination/src/application/planning-preview.service.ts`, `services/workflow-coordination/src/interface/http/planning-preview.controller.ts`, `tests/services/review-planning/artifact-execution.test.ts`, `services/workflow-coordination/src/application/planning-materialization.integration.test.ts`, and `services/workflow-coordination/src/interface/http/planning-preview.controller.test.ts` verify technical-planning artifacts persist preview/run metadata on TypeORM prototype rows and record append-only execution attempts/status through a service-owned Nest route without adding schema churn.

- This is only the planning gate/config/context/breakdown/continuous-update, generated technical plan review draft, current tRPC adapter, initial web/CLI/TUI entry-point layer, Nest approved-plan preview/materialize TypeORM routes, Nest freeform/guided start TypeORM routes, Nest continuous-update TypeORM route, and persisted artifact preview/execution records. W3 and W14 are partial, not final; W4 remains incomplete until a real prototype execution sandbox and final review UX are wired; W5/W6/W10/W11 remain incomplete until persisted ACP runtime sessions, full React review editor/dock UI, QA loops, React replacement wiring, automatic task reconciliation, broader service cutover, and TUI input/edit workflow controls are implemented. W12/W13 now have partial persisted handoff, decision, configured auto-decision, feedback scheduling, current-stack review workbench/session model/adapters, current-stack Svelte Plannotator-style review editor rendering for direct builds, persisted session loads, and persisted inline annotation appends, generated E2E artifact materialization, per-task/criterion coverage synthesis, Bun runner actions, Playwright runner planning, local CI generated-E2E wiring, and Nest TypeORM auto-decision/generated-E2E run route parity, but W12 still needs final React interactive inline annotation selection, real Dockview panels, and live AI-agent review UI.

### Fusion Dependency Ordering

- Fusion `packages/core/src/board.ts` copied/adapted into `src/application/orchestration/dependency-order.ts`.
- Copied/adapted dependency-order file includes provenance header with upstream repo, commit SHA, original path, license, local target path, and adaptation notes.
- `tests/upstream-derived/fusion/dependency-order.test.ts` preserves upstream-derived behavior for board column transitions, immutable transition copies, linear/diamond/complex dependency ordering, disconnected components, cycles, self-dependencies, and deterministic output.
- Fusion dependency-order, task-completion, and dashboard workflow dependency-disclosure semantics copied/adapted into `src/application/orchestration/dependency-run-preview.ts`.
- Copied/adapted dependency-run-preview file includes provenance header with upstream repo, commit SHA, original paths, license, local target path, and adaptation notes.
- `tests/upstream-derived/fusion/dependency-run-preview.test.ts` preserves upstream-derived behavior for full dependency-tree disclosure, dependency-before-dependent ordering, board multi-target previews, trace-linked preview scope, missing-dependency blocking, explicit-blocker warnings, and missing selected target reporting.
- Fusion dependency-run preview semantics are now connected to persisted Fulcrum task data through `src/application/orchestration/dependency-run-actions.ts`. The action loads real tasks/dependencies through application queries, maps Fulcrum statuses into Fusion preview columns, preserves trace IDs, and returns dependency-before-dependent disclosure rows.
- Current-stack adapters expose the persisted preview without moving business logic into UI surfaces: `apps/server/src/runtime/trpc/routers/tasks.ts` exposes `tasks.previewDependencyRun`; `apps/cli/src/product.ts` adds `fulcrum product tasks run-preview`; `apps/tui/src/screens/task-list.ts` and `apps/tui/src/index.ts` render task/board previews through the caller; `apps/web/src/routes/projects/[id]/board/+page.server.ts` adds a board action that supports selected board tasks including single-task target previews; `apps/web/src/routes/tasks/[id]/+page.server.ts` adds task-detail preview/dispatch actions over the same shared application service.
- The same shared action now dispatches dependency-tree runs by scheduling queueable `todo`/`triage` tasks in preview order, skipping satisfied `done`/`in-review`/`archived` tasks, refusing already `in-progress` included tasks, creating queued `agent_runs` and `jobs`, and writing a `dependency_tree_dispatched` event carrying trace, target, ordered, scheduled, and skipped IDs.
- Nest `workflows/copy-first/execution/dependency-run/dispatch` route/service now loads TypeORM tasks/dependencies, applies the same Fusion dependency order, queues `fulcrum_agent_runs`, skips already-satisfied tasks, refuses blocked previews, and writes a `fulcrum_run_events` dispatch audit row linked to the first queued run.

It repeatedly claims dependency/feedback jobs through the shared worker tick, records injected QA reviews through the shared QA action, includes QA-scheduled feedback runs in live feedback loading, builds default live reviewer-agent requests from task success criteria, completed run output, executor status, and trace events when no injected review callback exists, stops on approval/no queued work, blank/failed reviewer output, manual review, failed run, worker wait, or max iterations, emits `automated_feedback_loop_completed`, and is exposed through `tasks.runAutomatedFeedbackLoop` without moving logic into the tRPC surface. Tests: `services/execution-orchestration/src/application/automated-feedback-loop.integration.test.ts` and `tests/trpc/tasks-dependency-run-preview.test.ts`.
- This is only the dependency-order, dependency-run-preview, persisted preview/disclosure, queued dispatch scheduling, current-stack shared dependency worker tick, web board/task-detail run controls, task-detail dependency graph results, current-stack dependency-run live-feedback service/adapters, tRPC live-feedback stream, CLI watch stream, TUI live-feedback subscription, web task-detail EventSource live panel, current-stack automated feedback-loop exhaustion service/default reviewer execution/tRPC action, current-stack final-QA feedback-gate service/tRPC/web/CLI/TUI actions, Nest TypeORM dispatch scheduling, Nest TypeORM worker lifecycle-event recording, Nest TypeORM live dependency-run feedback loading, recoverable-failure retry, task-completion blocker, review-verdict, review-feed, persisted QA verdict action, Nest TypeORM QA feedback scheduling, final-QA report layer, UAT/code-review handoff layer, UAT/code-review decision layer, configured auto-decision layer, feedback-run scheduling, generated E2E artifact/file-materialization layer, generated E2E Bun runner layer, Playwright runner planning, per-task/criterion generated-E2E coverage synthesis, local CI generated-E2E wiring, current-stack Plannotator review workbench model/adapters, current-stack Svelte review editor rendering for direct builds, persisted session loads, and persisted annotation appends, persisted review-session storage/adapters, Nest TypeORM review-session save/load/annotate routes, and Nest TypeORM configured auto-decision/generated-E2E run routes. ### Server Persistence Strategy

- PostgreSQL and PGlite TypeORM migration strategy is written into `09.6-FULCRUM-INTEGRATION-MAP.md#postgresql-and-pglite-typeorm-migration-strategy`.
- The selected path keeps Nest/TypeORM defaults: `TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync(...)`, normal TypeORM `postgres` driver, `pg`, explicit migrations, and `synchronize: false`.
- PGlite local/test uses `@electric-sql/pglite-socket` so TypeORM connects through the same PostgreSQL wire protocol as production. `typeorm-pglite` is documented only as a fallback if the socket path hits a verified blocker.
- First implementation slice is in place: `apps/server/src/app.module.ts`, `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/database/typeorm-root.module.ts`, `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/database/typeorm-data-source.ts`, and `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/database/typeorm-foundation.test.ts` pin the Nest/TypeORM dependencies, expose `TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync(...)`, build normal TypeORM `postgres` options for both production PostgreSQL and PGlite socket URLs, keep `synchronize: false`, keep `migrationsRun: false`, and export a `DataSource` factory for CLI/test migration runners.
- First workflow-spine migration slice is in place: `services/workflow-coordination/src/infrastructure/database/workflow-spine.entities.ts` and `services/workflow-coordination/src/infrastructure/database/workflow-spine.migration.ts` create new Fulcrum-prefixed workspace/project/task/dependency/document/ACP-session/agent-run tables beside existing MikroORM tables, with trace IDs and JSON criteria/traffic/dependency-tree fields.
- `services/workflow-coordination/src/infrastructure/database/workflow-spine.migration.test.ts` starts a real `@electric-sql/pglite-socket` server, runs the TypeORM migration through the normal `pg`/Postgres driver path, and persists a representative freeform-doc -> task/dependency -> ACP session -> agent-run trace spine.
- Planning/review workflow migration slice is in place: `services/planning-review/src/infrastructure/database/review-workflow.entities.ts` and `services/planning-review/src/infrastructure/database/review-workflow.migration.ts` create new Fulcrum-prefixed artifact/plan/prototype/review-session/review-annotation/UAT-session/generated-E2E tables beside existing MikroORM tables.
- `services/planning-review/src/infrastructure/database/review-workflow.migration.test.ts` starts a real `@electric-sql/pglite-socket` server, runs the workflow-spine and review-workflow TypeORM migrations through the normal `pg`/Postgres driver path, and persists representative plan/prototype/review/UAT/generated-E2E data.
- `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-workflow-persistence.service.ts` is a Nest-owned TypeORM service wired through `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-workflow.module.ts` and `apps/server/src/app.module.ts`; `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-workflow-persistence.integration.test.ts` persists and reloads a representative freeform-doc -> planning/prototype -> dependency task execution -> review -> UAT -> generated-E2E trace cycle through real PGlite socket data.
- `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/database/typeorm-postgres.migration.test.ts` starts a real local PostgreSQL server through `tests/support/temporary-postgres.ts`, runs the workflow-spine and review-workflow TypeORM migrations, and persists/reloads the same representative copy-first trace cycle through `CopyFirstWorkflowPersistenceService`.
- `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-workflow.controller.ts` exposes the first Nest `workflows/copy-first` API boundary for persisting a cycle and loading trace summaries, with `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-workflow.controller.test.ts` covering route metadata, OpenAPI/class-validator boundary, module registration, and delegation into the TypeORM service.
- `services/work-management/src/infrastructure/database/work-structure.entities.ts` and `services/work-management/src/infrastructure/database/work-structure.migration.ts` add Plane-derived manual PM mirrors for states, labels, cycles, modules, saved views, intake, and notifications; `services/work-management/src/infrastructure/database/work-structure.migration.test.ts` proves those mirrors on both PGlite socket and real PostgreSQL.
- `services/knowledge-workspace/src/infrastructure/database/document.entities.ts` and `services/knowledge-workspace/src/infrastructure/database/document.migration.ts` add Docmost-derived docs/collaboration/search mirrors for pages, page history, comments, attachments, backlinks, collaboration state, and deterministic search entries; `services/knowledge-workspace/src/infrastructure/database/document.migration.test.ts` proves those mirrors on both PGlite socket and real PostgreSQL.
- `services/execution-orchestration/src/infrastructure/database/run-context.entities.ts` and `services/execution-orchestration/src/infrastructure/database/run-context.migration.ts` add context/memory/run-event mirrors for deterministic context bundles, durable memories, memory links, and ordered Fusion-style run events; `services/execution-orchestration/src/infrastructure/database/run-context.migration.test.ts` proves those mirrors on both PGlite socket and real PostgreSQL.
- `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-context.service.ts` and `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-context.controller.ts` add a Nest context trace route/service over the context/memory/run-event mirrors; `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-context.service.integration.test.ts` and `apps/server/src/workflows/copy-first-context.controller.test.ts` prove server-owned persistence and API delegation.
- This is still partial. Remaining broader Nest API route cutover, final web/CLI/TUI callers through Nest services, future TypeORM domain mirrors after new copied slices, and legacy MikroORM/Kysely removal remain incomplete.

## Completion Gate

### Current Service/DDD Structure

- Runtime workflow code is no longer organized by upstream/source-product or progress labels. The current bounded services are:
  - `services/agent-client-protocol/src/domain/**` and `services/agent-client-protocol/src/application/**`
  - `services/identity-access/src/application/**`
  - `services/integration-hub/src/application/**`
  - `services/knowledge-workspace/src/domain/**`, `services/knowledge-workspace/src/application/**`, and `services/knowledge-workspace/src/infrastructure/database/**`
  - `services/notification-center/src/application/**`
  - `services/work-management/src/domain/**`, `services/work-management/src/application/**`, and `services/work-management/src/infrastructure/database/**`
  - `services/planning-review/src/application/**`, `services/planning-review/src/application/reports/**`, `services/planning-review/src/application/reviews/**`, and `services/planning-review/src/infrastructure/database/**`
  - `services/platform-core/src/application/**`
  - `services/execution-orchestration/src/domain/**`, `services/execution-orchestration/src/application/**`, and `services/execution-orchestration/src/infrastructure/database/**`
  - `services/workflow-coordination/src/application/**`, `services/workflow-coordination/src/interface/http/**`, and `services/workflow-coordination/src/infrastructure/database/**`
- Domain folders own pure contracts and behavior primitives. Application folders own use cases/orchestration and may depend on domain; domain folders must not import application code.
- `apps/server` is now server composition/compatibility wiring for Nest plus existing callers. TypeORM bootstrapping lives under `services/platform-core/src/infrastructure/database/**`; service-owned EntitySchema and migration slices live under the owning service's `infrastructure/database` boundary.
- Planning/review report workflow contracts are owned by `services/planning-review/src/application/reports/report-contracts.ts`; legacy imports from `src/application/reports/types.ts` are compatibility-only and must not be used inside service implementations.
- Work-management report dashboard queries and narration sidecar client are owned by `services/work-management/src/application/report-dashboard.ts` and `services/work-management/src/application/inference-client.ts`. `src/application/reports/web-queries.ts` and `src/application/reports/inference-client.ts` are compatibility re-exports only.
- Web, CLI, and TUI continue to call application/service APIs only. They must not own copied workflow logic, direct ORM queries, TypeORM EntitySchema definitions, or Nest persistence wiring.
- Product/source inspiration names are allowed in this document, upstream inventory, copy/adapt matrix, and real external integration boundaries such as the Plane importer/connector. They are not acceptable in ordinary runtime/test file names, symbols, code comments, or UI labels.
- `tests/infrastructure/responsibility-first-naming.test.ts` is the guard for this rule. It scans `apps/**`, `services/**`, and `tests/**`, allowing third-party names only in explicit integration boundaries.
- Provenance moved out of runtime code and into the planning/copy matrix docs. Runtime naming is responsibility-first; source-product attribution belongs in docs and copied-source inventory.

- 2026-05-15 repo/settings/memory/context API-routing skip audit logged. Repo file browsing is intentionally unchanged because the current repository public API has no file tree or file detail contract. Repo branches are intentionally unchanged because the current public API lacks create/checkout/delete branch commands. Repo commit list/detail is intentionally unchanged because the current public API exposes stored commit read-model rows, while the web pages preserve real git pagination, totals, rendered diffs, and git fallback behavior. Settings feature flags are intentionally unchanged because cohort-rule editing is not exposed by the public feature-flag API. Settings backup/data/secrets are intentionally unchanged because public APIs do not yet preserve backup history, uploaded JSON import/export flow, or id-targeted secret actions without behavior loss. Settings importers are intentionally unchanged because the data-portability public API is not the same contract as the importer wizard's feature-gated CSV/API-key preflight and explicit unconfigured-execution behavior. Orchestration settings are intentionally unchanged because current public APIs do not expose orchestration config/workflow-definition CRUD, and that persistence is active ORM/Nest migration-lane work. Memory index/detail routes are intentionally unchanged because the current public memory rows do not preserve the existing page's `key` field and route/detail links depend on it. Context preview is intentionally unchanged because the public context endpoint covers bundle preview but the page also needs project/task selector options from application queries; converting only the bundle would leave mixed business-query ownership in the route.
